### HSQLManager

Provides centralized management of HSQLDB connections and operations
for StarMade databases with a modular architecture.

[Source](../../src/core/HSQLManager.ts#L270)

```typescript
export class HSQLManager {
```

### HSQLManager.constructor

Creates a new HSQLManager instance.

[Source](../../src/core/HSQLManager.ts#L341)

```typescript
constructor(config: HSQLManagerConfiguration)
```

- **@param config** - The configuration for the manager.

### HSQLManager.initialize

Initializes the manager, validates the world, and loads all configured modules.

[Source](../../src/core/HSQLManager.ts#L463)

```typescript
public async initialize(): Promise<void>
```

- **@returns** A promise that resolves when initialization is complete.

- **@throws** If the manager has already been destroyed.

- **@throws** If the manager is not in the UNINITIALIZED state.

- **@throws** If initialization fails for any reason.

### HSQLManager.getStatus

Gets the current status of the manager.

[Source](../../src/core/HSQLManager.ts#L990)

```typescript
public getStatus(): ManagerStatus
```

- **@returns** An object containing the current status information.

### HSQLManager.getConfiguration

Gets an isolated snapshot of the manager configuration with a frozen top-level object.

[Source](../../src/core/HSQLManager.ts#L1006)

```typescript
public getConfiguration(): Readonly<Required<HSQLManagerConfiguration>>
```

- **@returns** A detached configuration snapshot; changing nested values does not affect the manager.

### HSQLManager.getDatabaseUrl

Generates the HSQLDB connection URL for the configured world.

[Source](../../src/core/HSQLManager.ts#L1014)

```typescript
public getDatabaseUrl(): string
```

- **@returns** The database connection URL.

### HSQLManager.getModule

Gets a registered module by its name.

[Source](../../src/core/HSQLManager.ts#L1043)

```typescript
public getModule<T extends BaseModule>(name: string): T | undefined
```

- **@param name** - The name of the module.

- **@returns** The module instance, or undefined if not found.

### HSQLManager.registerModule

Registers a new module with the manager.

[Source](../../src/core/HSQLManager.ts#L1052)

```typescript
public registerModule(module: BaseModule): void
```

- **@param module** - The module instance to register.

- **@throws** If a module with the same name is already registered.

### HSQLManager.unregisterModule

Unregisters a module from the manager and calls its destroy method.

[Source](../../src/core/HSQLManager.ts#L1076)

```typescript
public async unregisterModule(name: string): Promise<void>
```

- **@param name** - The name of the module to unregister.

- **@returns** A promise that resolves when the module is unregistered.

- **@throws** If the module's destroy method fails.

### HSQLManager.destroy

Destroys the manager and all its registered modules, cleaning up all resources.
Modules are destroyed in the reverse order of their initialization (LIFO).

[Source](../../src/core/HSQLManager.ts#L1148)

```typescript
public async destroy(): Promise<void>
```

- **@returns** A promise that resolves when destruction is complete.

- **@throws** If the destruction process fails.

### HSQLManager.create

A static factory method to create and initialize an HSQLManager instance.

[Source](../../src/core/HSQLManager.ts#L1283)

```typescript
public static async create(config: HSQLManagerConfiguration): Promise<HSQLManager>
```

- **@param config** - The configuration for the manager.

- **@returns** A promise that resolves with the fully initialized manager.

### HSQLManager.healthCheck

Performs a health check on the manager and its modules.

[Source](../../src/core/HSQLManager.ts#L1294)

```typescript
public async healthCheck(): Promise<{
        healthy: boolean;
        status: ManagerStatus;
```

### forwardEvents

Forwards a specified list of events from a source emitter to a target emitter.
Can optionally add a prefix to the event names on the target emitter.

[Source](../../src/core/events.ts#L563)

```typescript
export function forwardEvents<T extends string | number | symbol>(
    source: ModuleEventEmitter<T>,
    target: ModuleEventEmitter<T>,
    events: T[],
    prefix?: string
): () => void
```

- **@param source** - The source event emitter.

- **@param target** - The target event emitter.

- **@param events** - An array of event names to forward.

- **@param prefix** - An optional prefix to add to the event names on the target.

- **@returns** A cleanup function that removes all the forwarding listeners.


### CacheManager.get

Retrieves a value from the cache by its key. This operation performs TTL checking
and updates LRU tracking information.

[Source](../../src/core/modules/cache/CacheManager.ts#L389)

```typescript
public async get<T = any>(key: string): Promise<T | undefined>
```

- **@param key** - The key of the item to retrieve.

- **@returns** The cached value, or undefined if not found or expired.

- **@throws** If the manager is not initialized.

### CacheManager.set

Stores a value in the cache. This operation handles memory limits by triggering
eviction if necessary and sets metadata like TTL.

[Source](../../src/core/modules/cache/CacheManager.ts#L453)

```typescript
public async set<T = any>(key: string, value: T, options?: CacheOptions): Promise<void>
```

- **@param key** - The key to store the value under.

- **@param value** - The value to cache.

- **@param options** - Optional settings for this cache entry, like TTL and tags.

- **@returns** A promise that resolves when the value is successfully cached.

- **@throws** If the manager is not initialized.

- **@throws** If the value cannot be cached for some reason.


- **@returns** True if the key exists and is valid, false otherwise.

- **@throws** If the manager is not initialized.


### CacheOptimizer.applyRecommendation

Reports that a recommendation requires manual cache configuration.
CacheManager currently supports LRU eviction and exposes no runtime strategy,
TTL-policy or memory-limit mutation API, so recommendations are advisory.

[Source](../../src/core/modules/cache/CacheOptimizer.ts#L938)

```typescript
public async applyRecommendation(recommendation: OptimizationRecommendation): Promise<boolean>
```

- **@param recommendation** - Recommendation to inspect without mutating the cache.

- **@returns** False: no cache configuration was changed.

- **@throws** If the optimizer is not initialized.

### ConnectionState

Defines the possible states of a connection.

[Source](../../src/core/modules/connection/ConnectionManager.ts#L49)

```typescript
export enum ConnectionState {
```

### ConnectionState.DISCONNECTED

The connection is not established.

[Source](../../src/core/modules/connection/ConnectionManager.ts#L51)

```typescript
DISCONNECTED = 'disconnected'
```

### ConnectionState.CONNECTING

The connection is in the process of being established.

[Source](../../src/core/modules/connection/ConnectionManager.ts#L53)

```typescript
CONNECTING = 'connecting'
```

### ConnectionState.CONNECTED

The connection is established and ready for use.

[Source](../../src/core/modules/connection/ConnectionManager.ts#L55)

```typescript
CONNECTED = 'connected'
```

### ConnectionState.RECONNECTING

The connection was lost and is attempting to reconnect.

[Source](../../src/core/modules/connection/ConnectionManager.ts#L57)

```typescript
RECONNECTING = 'reconnecting'
```

### ConnectionState.CLOSING

The connection is in the process of being closed.

[Source](../../src/core/modules/connection/ConnectionManager.ts#L59)

```typescript
CLOSING = 'closing'
```

### ConnectionState.FAILED

The connection has failed and cannot be used.

[Source](../../src/core/modules/connection/ConnectionManager.ts#L61)

```typescript
FAILED = 'failed'
```

### ConnectionManager

and automatic recovery capabilities using JDBCConnectionFactory.

```typescript
export class ConnectionManager implements BaseModule, ModuleEventEmitter<ConnectionEvent> {
```

```typescript
constructor()
```

### ConnectionManager.initialize

Initializes the connection manager. This sets up the configuration,
initializes the JDBC factory, creates the initial connection pool, and
starts monitoring and recovery processes.

[Source](../../src/core/modules/connection/ConnectionManager.ts#L342)

```typescript
public async initialize(manager: HSQLManager): Promise<void>
```

- **@param manager** - The HSQLDB manager instance.

- **@returns** A promise that resolves when initialization is complete.

- **@throws** If the module is already initialized.

- **@throws** If the provided manager is invalid.

- **@throws** If the JDBC factory or connection pool fails to initialize.

### ConnectionManager.getConnection

Retrieves a JDBC connection. If pooling is enabled, it will attempt to reuse
an available connection from the pool or create a new one if space is available.
If the pool is full, it will wait for a connection to become available.

[Source](../../src/core/modules/connection/ConnectionManager.ts#L863)

```typescript
public async getConnection(): Promise<JDBCConnection>
```

- **@returns** A ready-to-use JDBC connection.

- **@throws** If the manager is not initialized.

- **@throws** If creating a new connection fails.

- **@throws** If waiting for a connection times out.

### ConnectionManager.getTransactionConnection

Gets a connection for a transaction session - ensures same connection is used

[Source](../../src/core/modules/connection/ConnectionManager.ts#L953)

```typescript
public async getTransactionConnection(sessionId?: string): Promise<{ connection: JDBCConnection, sessionId: string }>
```

- **@deprecated** This method is no longer needed with the refactored TransactionManager v2.0

- **@param sessionId** - Optional session identifier for transaction continuity

- **@returns** Connection with session info

- **@throws** If the manager is not initialized.

- **@throws** If creating a new connection fails.

- **@throws** If waiting for a connection times out.

### ConnectionManager.releaseTransactionSession

Releases a transaction session and its connection

[Source](../../src/core/modules/connection/ConnectionManager.ts#L992)

```typescript
public async releaseTransactionSession(sessionId: string): Promise<void>
```

- **@deprecated** This method is no longer needed with the refactored TransactionManager v2.0

- **@param sessionId** - The session identifier

- **@returns** 

### ConnectionManager.releaseConnection

Releases a JDBC connection. If pooling is enabled, the connection is returned
to the pool. If pooling is disabled, the connection is closed.

[Source](../../src/core/modules/connection/ConnectionManager.ts#L1225)

```typescript
public async releaseConnection(jdbcConnection: JDBCConnection): Promise<void>
```

- **@param jdbcConnection** - The JDBC connection to release.

- **@returns** A promise that resolves when the connection is released.


- **@returns** Cleanup operation promise.

- **@throws** After draining all pools when one or more physical sessions fail to close.

### JDBCConnectionFactory.forceProcessCleanup

Schedule process termination for applications whose JVM keeps Node alive.
The shared JVM remains available until the process exits.

[Source](../../src/core/modules/connection/JDBCConnectionFactory.ts#L1256)

```typescript
public static async forceProcessCleanup(exitCode: number = 0, delayMs: number = 1000): Promise<void>
```

- **@param exitCode** - Process exit code.

- **@param delayMs** - Grace period; termination uses half this delay for compatibility.

- **@returns** Resolves once the termination timer is scheduled.

Measures the execution time of a function and returns both the result and duration.

[Source](../../src/core/modules/performance/PerformanceMonitor.ts#L1414)

```typescript

```

- **@returns** Object containing the result and duration in milliseconds.

- **@throws** Re-throws any error from the function with duration information added.

```typescript
constructor()
```

### ParameterizedQuery.initialize

Initialize the parameterized query module

[Source](../../src/core/modules/query/ParameterizedQuery.ts#L482)

```typescript
public async initialize(manager: HSQLManager): Promise<void>
```

- **@param manager** - The HSQLDB manager instance

- **@returns** Promise that resolves when initialization is complete

- **@throws** If already initialized

- **@throws** If manager parameter is null or undefined

### ParameterizedQuery.execute

Execute a parameterized query with positional parameters

[Source](../../src/core/modules/query/ParameterizedQuery.ts#L631)

```typescript
public async execute(
        sql: string,
        parameters: any[] = [],
        options: QueryExecutionOptions = {}
    ): Promise<ParameterizedQueryResult>
```

- **@param sql** - SQL query with ? placeholders

- **@param parameters** - Array of parameter values

- **@param options** - Execution options

- **@returns** Query execution result

- **@throws** If module is not initialized

- **@throws** If parameters are invalid

- **@throws** If query execution fails

- **@example** ```typescript const result = await paramQuery.execute(   'SELECT * FROM PLAYERS WHERE name = ? AND level > ?',   ['player1', 10] ); ```

### ParameterizedQuery.executeNamed

Execute a parameterized query with named parameters

[Source](../../src/core/modules/query/ParameterizedQuery.ts#L701)

```typescript
public async executeNamed(
        sql: string,
        parameters: NamedParameterMap = {},
        options: QueryExecutionOptions = {}
    ): Promise<ParameterizedQueryResult>
```

- **@param sql** - SQL query with :name placeholders

- **@param parameters** - Map of parameter names to values

- **@param options** - Execution options

- **@returns** Query execution result

- **@throws** If module is not initialized

- **@throws** If parameters are invalid

- **@throws** If query execution fails

- **@example** ```typescript const result = await paramQuery.executeNamed(   'SELECT * FROM PLAYERS WHERE name = :name AND level > :minLevel',   { name: 'player1', minLevel: 10 } ); ```

### ParameterizedQuery.executeBatch

Execute a batch of parameterized queries

[Source](../../src/core/modules/query/ParameterizedQuery.ts#L747)

```typescript
public async executeBatch(
        sql: string,
        batchParameters: BatchParameters,
        options: QueryExecutionOptions = {}
    ): Promise<BatchExecutionResult>
```

- **@param sql** - SQL query template

- **@param batchParameters** - Array of parameter arrays

- **@param options** - Execution options

- **@returns** Batch execution result

- **@throws** If module is not initialized

- **@throws** If batch parameters are invalid

- **@throws** If batch execution fails

- **@example** ```typescript const result = await paramQuery.executeBatch(   'INSERT INTO PLAYERS (name, level) VALUES (?, ?)',   [     ['player1', 10],     ['player2', 15],     ['player3', 20]   ] ); ```

### QueryExecutor

QueryExecutor - Core SQL query execution engine

Features:
- Connection pooling integration
- Query result caching
- Performance monitoring
- Error handling and recovery
- Query tracking and statistics
- Event emission for metrics

### QueryValidator

QueryValidator - SQL query validation and security analysis engine

Features:
- SQL syntax validation
- SQL injection detection
- Security threat analysis
- Performance optimization hints
- Query complexity analysis
- Schema validation
- Query rewriting suggestions
- Validation result caching

[Source](../../src/core/modules/query/QueryValidator.ts#L392)

```typescript
export class QueryValidator implements BaseModule, ModuleEventEmitter<ModuleEvent> {
```

### SchemaAnalyzer

Schema Analyzer

Core database schema analysis and metadata extraction system that provides
insights into database structure and basic optimization opportunities.

**Core Features:**
- Real JDBC metadata extraction from HSQLDB
- Table and column structure analysis
- Index and constraint discovery
- Basic statistical analysis and health assessment
- Performance optimization recommendations

**Focused Responsibilities:**
- Schema metadata extraction only
- Basic structural analysis and validation
- Simple optimization recommendations

[Source](../../src/core/modules/schema/SchemaAnalyzer.ts#L474)

```typescript
export class SchemaAnalyzer implements BaseModule, ModuleEventEmitter<ModuleEvent> {
```

### SchemaAnalyzer.name

Stable module identifier used when registering and looking up the module.

[Source](../../src/core/modules/schema/SchemaAnalyzer.ts#L477)

```typescript
public readonly name = 'schema-analyzer';
```

### SchemaAnalyzer.version

Version of this module implementation.

[Source](../../src/core/modules/schema/SchemaAnalyzer.ts#L480)

```typescript
public readonly version = '1.0.0';
```

### SchemaAnalyzer.isInitialized

Whether initialization completed and the module is available for use.

[Source](../../src/core/modules/schema/SchemaAnalyzer.ts#L483)

```typescript
public get isInitialized(): boolean
```

### SchemaAnalyzer.constructor

Create a new SchemaAnalyzer instance

[Source](../../src/core/modules/schema/SchemaAnalyzer.ts#L531)

```typescript
constructor()
```

### SchemaAnalyzer.initialize

Initialize the schema analyzer

Sets up the schema analyzer with the provided HSQLManager instance,
configures analysis parameters, and establishes ConnectionManager integration.

[Source](../../src/core/modules/schema/SchemaAnalyzer.ts#L562)

```typescript
public async initialize(manager: HSQLManager): Promise<void>
```

- **@param manager** - The HSQLDB manager instance

- **@returns** Promise that resolves when initialization is complete

- **@throws** If already initialized

- **@throws** If manager parameter is null or undefined

- **@example** ```typescript const manager = new HSQLManager(config); await manager.initialize();  const schemaAnalyzer = new SchemaAnalyzer(); await schemaAnalyzer.initialize(manager); ```

### SchemaAnalyzer.getTableCount

Get table count from database using HSQLDB-compatible query

Fast method to get the number of tables in the database without full analysis.
Uses the corrected HSQLDB-compatible query for table enumeration.

CORRECTION FINALE: Filtrage précis des tables système HSQLDB tout en incluant SYSTEMS

[Source](../../src/core/modules/schema/SchemaAnalyzer.ts#L845)

```typescript
public async getTableCount(includeSystemTables: boolean = false): Promise<number>
```

- **@param includeSystemTables** - Whether to include system tables

- **@returns** Number of tables in the database

- **@throws** If the analyzer is not initialized

- **@throws** If database query fails

- **@example** ```typescript // Get count of user tables only const userTableCount = await schemaAnalyzer.getTableCount(); console.log(`Database has ${userTableCount} user tables`);  // Get count including system tables const allTableCount = await schemaAnalyzer.getTableCount(true); console.log(`Database has ${allTableCount} total tables`); ```

### SchemaAnalyzer.analyzeSchema

Analyze complete database schema

Performs a comprehensive analysis of the entire database schema using real JDBC metadata,
including all tables, columns, indexes, constraints, and basic health assessment.
Uses advanced caching with CacheManager when available for improved performance.

[Source](../../src/core/modules/schema/SchemaAnalyzer.ts#L1624)

```typescript
public async analyzeSchema(options?: Partial<SchemaAnalysisConfig>): Promise<DatabaseSchema>
```

- **@param options** - Analysis configuration options

- **@returns** Complete database schema information

- **@throws** If the analyzer is not initialized

- **@throws** If database queries fail

- **@throws** If database connection issues occur

- **@example** ```typescript // Basic schema analysis with real JDBC data const schema = await schemaAnalyzer.analyzeSchema(); console.log(`Found ${schema.tables.length} tables`);  // Deep analysis with statistics const detailedSchema = await schemaAnalyzer.analyzeSchema({   enableDeepAnalysis: true,   enableStatistics: true,   maxSampleSize: 5000,   includeSystemTables: false }); ```

### SchemaAnalyzer.clearCache

Clear analysis cache

Clears all cached analysis results to force fresh analysis on next request.
This includes both advanced cache (CacheManager) and internal cache.
This is useful when the database schema may have changed externally.

[Source](../../src/core/modules/schema/SchemaAnalyzer.ts#L2023)

```typescript
public async clearCache(pattern?: string): Promise<void>
```

- **@param pattern** - Optional pattern to match for selective clearing

- **@returns** 

- **@example** ```typescript // Clear all cache after schema modifications await executeSchemaChanges(); await schemaAnalyzer.clearCache();  // Clear specific table cache await schemaAnalyzer.clearCache('PLAYERS');  // Next analysis will be fresh const updatedSchema = await schemaAnalyzer.analyzeSchema(); ```

### SchemaAnalyzer.analyzeTable

Analyze a specific table in detail using real JDBC metadata

Performs comprehensive analysis of a single table including
column metadata, statistics, indexes, constraints, and relationships.
Uses advanced caching with CacheManager when available for improved performance.

[Source](../../src/core/modules/schema/SchemaAnalyzer.ts#L2074)

```typescript
public async analyzeTable(tableName: string, options?: Partial<SchemaAnalysisConfig>): Promise<TableInfo>
```

- **@param tableName** - Name of the table to analyze

- **@param options** - Analysis configuration options

- **@returns** Complete table analysis information

- **@throws** If the analyzer is not initialized

- **@throws** If database queries fail

- **@throws** If table does not exist

- **@example** ```typescript // Analyze PLAYERS table with real data const playersTable = await schemaAnalyzer.analyzeTable('PLAYERS'); console.log(`PLAYERS table has ${playersTable.columns.length} columns`); console.log(`Row count: ${playersTable.statistics.rowCount}`);  // Deep analysis with statistics const detailedAnalysis = await schemaAnalyzer.analyzeTable('ENTITIES', {   enableDeepAnalysis: true,   enableStatistics: true,   maxSampleSize: 10000 }); ```

### SchemaAnalyzer.getOptimizationRecommendations

Get basic optimization recommendations for the schema

Analyzes the schema structure and provides basic actionable
optimization recommendations focused on common performance issues.

[Source](../../src/core/modules/schema/SchemaAnalyzer.ts#L2250)

```typescript
public async getOptimizationRecommendations(tableNames?: string[]): Promise<OptimizationRecommendation[]>
```

- **@param tableNames** - Specific tables to analyze (optional, defaults to all tables)

- **@returns** Array of optimization recommendations

- **@throws** If the analyzer is not initialized

- **@example** ```typescript // Get recommendations for all tables const recommendations = await schemaAnalyzer.getOptimizationRecommendations(); console.log(`Found ${recommendations.length} optimization opportunities`);  recommendations.forEach(rec => {   console.log(`${rec.priority}: ${rec.title}`);   console.log(`  Description: ${rec.description}`);   console.log(`  Expected Benefit: ${rec.expectedBenefit}`);   console.log(`  Implementation Effort: ${rec.effort}`); }); ```

### TransactionContext.invalidate

Closes this context and its savepoints before its JDBC session is returned to the pool.
Called by the owning TransactionManager during completion or emergency cleanup.
This state change performs no logging or I/O so it cannot prevent session release.

```typescript
public invalidate(): void
```

## src/core/utils.ts

### detectStarMadeInstallation

Detects StarMade installations in common system paths or custom-provided paths.

[Source](../../src/core/utils.ts#L21)

```typescript
export async function detectStarMadeInstallation(searchPaths?: string[]): Promise<{
    found: boolean;
    path?: string;
    version?: string;
    availableWorlds: string[];
}>
```

- **@param searchPaths** - An optional array of paths to search for a StarMade installation. If not provided, default paths are used.

- **@returns** An object indicating if an installation was found, its path, version (if detectable), and a list of available worlds.

### detectAvailableWorlds

Detects available world directories within a given StarMade installation directory.

[Source](../../src/core/utils.ts#L66)

```typescript
export async function detectAvailableWorlds(starmadeDir: string): Promise<string[]>
```

- **@param starmadeDir** - The path to the StarMade installation directory.

- **@returns** A promise that resolves with a list of available world names.

### validateStarMadeDirectory

Validates that a given path points to a valid StarMade directory structure.

[Source](../../src/core/utils.ts#L94)

```typescript
export function validateStarMadeDirectory(starmadeDir: string): void
```

- **@param starmadeDir** - The path to the StarMade installation directory.

- **@throws** If the directory does not exist or lacks the 'server-database' subdirectory.

### formatDuration

Formats a duration in milliseconds into a human-readable string (e.g., "1.2s", "5.0m").

[Source](../../src/core/utils.ts#L110)

```typescript
export function formatDuration(ms: number): string
```

- **@param ms** - The duration in milliseconds.

- **@returns** The formatted duration string.

### generateDatabaseUrl

Generates the HSQLDB JDBC URL for a specific StarMade world database.

[Source](../../src/core/utils.ts#L133)

```typescript
export function generateDatabaseUrl(starmadeDir: string, worldName: string, options?: {
    readOnly?: boolean;
    ifExists?: boolean;
    shutdown?: boolean;
    disableLockFile?: boolean;
}): string
```

- **@param starmadeDir** - The path to the StarMade installation directory.

- **@param worldName** - The name of the world/database to connect to.

- **@param options** - Optional parameters for the database connection.

- **@returns** The fully formed JDBC URL for the StarMade database.

### isValidWorldDirectory

Checks if a given path is a valid StarMade world directory by looking for HSQLDB data files.

[Source](../../src/core/utils.ts#L177)

```typescript
export function isValidWorldDirectory(worldPath: string): boolean
```

- **@param worldPath** - The path to the potential world directory.

- **@returns** True if the directory is a valid StarMade world, false otherwise.

### resourceCleaner

A simple singleton object for managing cleanup of resources that need to be destroyed.

[Source](../../src/core/utils.ts#L206)

```typescript
export const resourceCleaner = {
```

### retry

An async utility function to retry a promise-returning function a specified number of times.

[Source](../../src/core/utils.ts#L254)

```typescript
export async function retry<T>(
    fn: () => Promise<T>,
    maxAttempts: number = 3,
    delay: number = 1000
): Promise<T>
```

- **@param fn** - The function to retry.

- **@param maxAttempts** - The maximum number of attempts.

- **@param delay** - The base delay in milliseconds between retries. The delay increases with each attempt.

- **@returns** A promise that resolves with the result of the function if it succeeds.

- **@throws** The last error encountered if all attempts fail.

### debounce

A utility function that creates a debounced version of a function.
The debounced function will only be called after it has not been called for a specified delay.

[Source](../../src/core/utils.ts#L285)

```typescript
export function debounce<T extends (...args: any[]) => any>(
    fn: T,
    delay: number
): (...args: Parameters<T>) => void
```

- **@param fn** - The function to debounce.

- **@param delay** - The debounce delay in milliseconds.

- **@returns** The debounced function.

### EntitiesModel.getEntitySummary

Get comprehensive entity summary with all relationship data

[Source](../../src/tables/entities/EntitiesModel.ts#L1251)

```typescript
public getEntitySummary(): {
        // Basic entity data
        id: number;
        uid: string;
        name?: string;
        type: EntityType;
        typeName: string;
        coordinates: string;
        faction: string;
        creator?: string;
        
        // Spatial intelligence
        sectorName?: string;
        
        // Docking intelligence
        docking: {
            isDocked: boolean;
            isRoot: boolean;
            dockedToName?: string;
            rootName?: string;
            dockedEntitiesCount: number;
        };
        
        // FTL intelligence
        ftl: {
            hasCapabilities: boolean;
            isHub: boolean;
            hasFrom: boolean;
            hasTo: boolean;
            hasBidirectional: boolean;
        };
        
        // Fleet intelligence
        fleet: {
            isActive: boolean;
            isCommander: boolean;
            involvement: {
                isMember: boolean;
                isFlagship: boolean;
                hasDockedMember: boolean;
            };
        };
        
        // Effects intelligence
        effects: {
            hasActive: boolean;
            count: number;
            byCategory: Record<string, number>;
        };
        
        // Relationship status
        relationshipStatus: {
            hasSectorLoaded: boolean;
            hasAllDockingRelationsLoaded: boolean;
            hasAllFtlRelationsLoaded: boolean;
            hasAllFleetRelationsLoaded: boolean;
            hasEffectsLoaded: boolean;
            hasAllRelationsLoaded: boolean;
        };
    }
```

- **@returns** Number of members in the fleet

- **@throws** If the query fails

```typescript
        isDocked: boolean;
        dockedToId: number;
        dockedToName?: string;
        dockingDepth: number; // Always 1 for this member, but useful for consistency
        chainLength: number; // Known length from this member down
        recommendations: string[];
    }
```

### FleetsModel.decodeCommand

Decodes FLEETS.COMMAND into a typed FleetCommandObject.

Returns null when the column is empty; malformed bytes raise DecodeError.
Requires starmade-decoder to be installed.

[Source](../../src/tables/fleets/FleetsModel.ts#L1117)

```typescript
public decodeCommand(): import('starmade-decoder').FleetCommandObject | null
```

- **@example** const cmd = fleet.decodeCommand(); if (cmd) console.log(cmd.commandType, cmd.firstVec3iArg);

### FleetsModel.decodeRemotes

Decodes FLEETS.SAVED_REMOTES into a typed FleetRemotesObject.

Handles both the network DataOutput format and the Java ObjectOutputStream
format (AC ED magic). Returns an empty object for null/empty input.

[Source](../../src/tables/fleets/FleetsModel.ts#L1134)

```typescript
public decodeRemotes(): import('starmade-decoder').FleetRemotesObject
```

- **@example** const remotes = fleet.decodeRemotes(); console.log(remotes.activeNames); // ['door_alpha', ...]

### FleetsModel.encodeCommand

Encodes and stores FLEETS.COMMAND from a typed FleetCommandObject or raw
FleetCommand structure.

Passing null/undefined clears the column. Object inputs are padded to 1024
bytes by default, matching the StarMade Java VARBINARY size.

[Source](../../src/tables/fleets/FleetsModel.ts#L1151)

```typescript
public encodeCommand(
        command: import('starmade-decoder').FleetCommandObject | import('starmade-decoder').FleetCommand | null | undefined,
        padTo = 1024
    ): this
```

- **@example** const { FleetCommandObject } = decoder; fleet.encodeCommand(FleetCommandObject.create(42n, 'IDLE'));

### FleetsModel.encodeRemotes

Encodes and stores FLEETS.SAVED_REMOTES from a FleetRemotesObject, Map, or
plain record. The writer uses the Java ObjectOutputStream database format,
including for objects originally decoded from network bytes. Incomplete
recovery objects raise DecodeError without replacing the stored value.

Passing null/undefined clears the column.

[Source](../../src/tables/fleets/FleetsModel.ts#L1177)

```typescript
public encodeRemotes(
        remotes: import('starmade-decoder').FleetRemotesObject | Map<string, boolean> | Record<string, boolean> | null | undefined
    ): this
```

- **@example** const remotes = fleet.decodeRemotes().withRemote('door_alpha', true); fleet.encodeRemotes(remotes);

### PlayerMessagesModel.generateMessageAnalytics

[Source](../../src/tables/player-messages/PlayerMessagesModel.ts#L549)

```typescript
public generateMessageAnalytics(): {
        basic: {
            id: number;
            from: string;
            to: string;
            subject: string;
            preview: string;
            sent: string;
            relativeTime: string;
            isRead: boolean;
            hasAttachment: boolean;
        };
        classification: {
            priority: ReturnType<PlayerMessagesModel['getMessagePriority']>;
            category: ReturnType<PlayerMessagesModel['getMessageCategory']>;
            isRecent: boolean;
            isSelfMessage: boolean;
            isInterFaction: boolean;
            isAdminMessage: boolean;
        };
        players: {
            intelligence: ReturnType<PlayerMessagesModel['getPlayersIntelligence']>;
            hasPlayersLoaded: boolean;
        };
        metrics: {
            ageMs: number;
            messageLength: number;
            topicLength: number;
            wordCount: number;
        };
        insights: string[];
    }
```

```typescript
public getMessageSummary(): {
        id: number;
        from: string;
        to: string;
        subject: string;
        preview: string;
        sent: string;
        relativeTime: string;
        isRead: boolean;
        hasAttachment: boolean;
        // Enhanced data
        priority: ReturnType<PlayerMessagesModel['getMessagePriority']>;
        category: ReturnType<PlayerMessagesModel['getMessageCategory']>;
        hasPlayersLoaded: boolean;
        senderIntelligence?: ReturnType<PlayerMessagesModel['getPlayersIntelligence']>['sender'];
        receiverIntelligence?: ReturnType<PlayerMessagesModel['getPlayersIntelligence']>['receiver'];
        isInterFaction?: boolean;
        isAdminMessage?: boolean;
    }
```

### PlayersModel.generatePlayerProfile

Generate comprehensive player profile

[Source](../../src/tables/players/PlayersModel.ts#L667)

```typescript
public generatePlayerProfile(): {
        basic: {
            id: number;
            name: string;
            starmadeName: string;
            faction: number;
            isInFaction: boolean;
            role: string;
            displayName: string;
        };
        permissions: {
            permission: number;
            role: string;
            permissionNames: string[];
            isAdmin: boolean;
            isOfficer: boolean;
            isLeader: boolean;
        };
        assets: {
            sentMessages?: number;
            receivedMessages?: number;
            totalMessages?: number;
            ownedMines?: number;
            ownedTradeNodes?: number;
            economicAssets?: number;
            ownedFleets?: number;
            createdEntities?: number;
            modifiedEntities?: number;
            totalEntityInteractions?: number;
            activityScore?: number;
        };
        analytics: {
            activity?: ReturnType<PlayersModel['getActivityAnalysis']>;
            influence?: ReturnType<PlayersModel['getInfluenceAssessment']>;
        };
        relationshipStatus: {
            hasMessagesLoaded: boolean;
            hasEconomicAssetsLoaded: boolean;
            hasOwnedFleetsLoaded: boolean;
            hasEntitiesLoaded: boolean;
            hasAllAssetsLoaded: boolean;
        };
        overallRating: 'NEWCOMER' | 'DEVELOPING' | 'ESTABLISHED' | 'VETERAN' | 'LEGEND';
    }
```

### MAX_ITEMS_SIZE

### ITEM_RECORD_SIZE

Size of each item record in bytes

```typescript

```

### MAX_ITEM_STACKS

Maximum number of item stacks per sector

[Source](../../src/tables/sectors-items/SectorsItemsModel.ts#L41)

```typescript
export const MAX_ITEM_STACKS = Math.floor(MAX_ITEMS_SIZE / ITEM_RECORD_SIZE);
```

### SectorsItemsModel

Model for the SECTORS_ITEMS table

Stores serialized binary data for items floating freely in space sectors.
Each record contains a fixed-size sequence of item stacks with position and metadata.

[Source](../../src/tables/sectors-items/SectorsItemsModel.ts#L53)

### SectorsItemsModel.decodeItems

Decodes SECTORS_ITEMS.ITEMS into a typed SectorItemsObject.

Each item stack exposes: blockType, count, posX/Y/Z (local sector coords),
metaId. Returns an empty object for null/empty data.

[Source](../../src/tables/sectors-items/SectorsItemsModel.ts#L380)

```typescript
public decodeItems(): import('starmade-decoder').SectorItemsObject
```

- **@example** const items = record.decodeItems(); console.log(items.size);            // number of stacks console.log(items.types);           // distinct block type IDs console.log(items.totalCount(259)); // total Faction Module count

### SectorsItemsModel.encodeItems

Encodes and stores SECTORS_ITEMS.ITEMS from a SectorItemsObject or a raw
FreeItem array.

[Source](../../src/tables/sectors-items/SectorsItemsModel.ts#L394)

```typescript
public encodeItems(
        items: import('starmade-decoder').SectorItemsObject | import('starmade-decoder').FreeItem[]
    ): this
```

- **@example** const items = record.decodeItems().withMerged(259); record.encodeItems(items);

### SectorsModel.getSectorSummary

Get ultimate sector summary with all information

[Source](../../src/tables/sectors/SectorsModel.ts#L987)

```typescript
public getSectorSummary(): {
        // Basic Information
        id: number;
        coordinates: string;
        type: SectorType;
        typeName: string;
        name: string;
        stellar: number;
        protection: number;
        protectionDescription: string;
        transient: boolean;
        lastReplenished: number;
        itemsReference: number;
        
        // Relationship Counts (if loaded)
        entitiesCount?: number;
        tradeNodesCount?: number;
        minesCount?: number;
        visibilityRecordsCount?: number;
        ftlRoutesCount?: { from: number; to: number; total: number };
        
        // Relationship Status
        hasSystemLoaded?: boolean;
        hasSectorItemsLoaded?: boolean;
        hasCriticalRelationsLoaded?: boolean;
        hasEconomicRelationsLoaded?: boolean;
        
        // Advanced Analytics
        economicActivityScore?: number;
        totalActivityScore?: number;
        sectorAnalysis?: SectorAnalysis;
        classification?: {
            category: string;
            confidence: number;
            reasoning: string[];
        };
        protectionAnalysis?: {
            level: ProtectionLevel;
            isSecure: boolean;
            canModify: boolean;
            recommendations: string[];
        };
        
        // Performance Metrics
        performanceScore?: number;
        lastCalculated?: number;
    }
```

### SectorsModel.calculateRelativeCoordinatesInSystem

Derive system coordinates and local coordinates from global sector positions.

[Source](../../src/tables/sectors/SectorsModel.ts#L1203)

```typescript
public calculateRelativeCoordinatesInSystem(systemSize: number = 16): {
        relative: { x: number; y: number; z: number };
        systemCoords: { x: number; y: number; z: number };
        calculated: boolean;
    }
```

- **@param systemSize** Number of sectors per axis, sixteen in the StarMade grid.

- **@returns** Nonnegative local coordinates and floor-divided system coordinates.

- **@throws** RangeError When the grid size is not a positive safe integer.

### SectorsModel.isSystemOrigin

Test whether this sector lies at the local origin of its system grid.

[Source](../../src/tables/sectors/SectorsModel.ts#L1232)

```typescript
public isSystemOrigin(systemSize: number = 16): boolean
```

- **@param systemSize** Number of sectors per axis; defaults to sixteen.

- **@returns** True when every local coordinate is zero.

### MAX_INFOS_SIZE

Maximum size for INFOS binary data as per StarMade database specification

[Source](../../src/tables/systems/SystemsModel.ts#L34)

```typescript

```

### MAX_RESOURCES_SIZE

Maximum size for RESOURCES binary data as per StarMade database specification.
The extended StarMade-Open schema uses 19 bytes; the supplied game/fixture uses 16.

[Source](../../src/tables/systems/SystemsModel.ts#L40)

```typescript
export const MAX_RESOURCES_SIZE = 19;
```

The decoder exposes 19 resource slots, including three absent in the legacy schema.

```typescript

```

### SystemsModel.decodeStarSystem

Decodes SYSTEMS.INFOS + SYSTEMS.RESOURCES into a typed StarSystem object.

INFOS: 16³ × 2-byte grid of sector types and metadata (SectorType enum +
PlanetType for planet sectors). RESOURCES: 19-byte resource density array
indexed by ElementKeyMap.resources.

Returns null when INFOS is absent/empty. Malformed nonempty cells raise DecodeError.
Legacy 16-byte resource cells are expanded with three absent resource slots
for decoding only; the stored bytes are preserved.

[Source](../../src/tables/systems/SystemsModel.ts#L776)

```typescript
public decodeStarSystem(): import('starmade-decoder').StarSystem | null
```

- **@example** const sys = system.decodeStarSystem(); if (sys) {   console.log(sys.toString());          // StarSystem(sun=1, planets=3, ...)   console.log(sys.planets);             // SectorInfo[] for all planet sectors   console.log(sys.presentResources);    // SystemResource[] with density > 0   system.encodeStarSystem(             // preserve the target database layout     sys.withResourceDensity(0, 80));    // reject any lossy legacy conversion }

### SystemsModel.decodeResources

Decodes only SYSTEMS.RESOURCES into a typed array of SystemResource.

Convenience shortcut when sector grid data is not needed.
Returns resources with density > 0 by default.

[Source](../../src/tables/systems/SystemsModel.ts#L793)

```typescript
public decodeResources(): import('starmade-decoder').SystemResource[]
```

- **@example** const res = system.decodeResources(); res.forEach(r => console.log(r.name, r.density)); // 'Hattel Crystal', 80

### SystemsModel.encodeStarSystem

Encodes and stores SYSTEMS.INFOS + SYSTEMS.RESOURCES from a StarSystem
business object. Defaults to the supplied database's 16-byte layout.

[Source](../../src/tables/systems/SystemsModel.ts#L811)

```typescript
public encodeStarSystem(system: import('starmade-decoder').StarSystem, resourceSize: 16 | 19 = 16): this
```

- **@param system** Typed system to encode.

- **@param resourceSize** Target database column width: 16 (legacy) or 19 (extended).

- **@throws** If the width is unsupported or a legacy write would lose resources.

- **@example** const updated = system.decodeStarSystem()?.withResourceDensity(0, 80); if (updated) system.encodeStarSystem(updated);

### SystemsModel.encodeInfos

Encodes and stores SYSTEMS.INFOS from typed sector entries.

[Source](../../src/tables/systems/SystemsModel.ts#L821)

```typescript
public encodeInfos(infos: import('starmade-decoder').SectorInfo[]): this
```

### SystemsModel.encodeResources

Encodes and stores SYSTEMS.RESOURCES from typed resource density entries.

[Source](../../src/tables/systems/SystemsModel.ts#L832)

```typescript
public encodeResources(resources: import('starmade-decoder').SystemResource[], resourceSize: 16 | 19 = 16): this
```

- **@param resources** Densities to encode.

- **@param resourceSize** Target database column width: 16 (legacy) or 19 (extended).

- **@throws** If the width is unsupported or a legacy write would lose resources.

### TradeNodesModel.generateTradingReport

Generate comprehensive trading intelligence report

[Source](../../src/tables/trade-nodes/TradeNodesModel.ts#L681)

```typescript
public generateTradingReport(): {
        basic: {
            id: number;
            name: string;
            coordinates: string;
            owner: string;
            faction: string;
            permissionLevel: string;
        };
        economics: {
            credits: number;
            formattedCredits: string;
            capacity: number;
            volume: number;
            utilization: number;
            financialStatus: string;
            storageStatus: string;
        };
        intelligence: {
            sector: ReturnType<TradeNodesModel['getSectorIntelligence']>;
            player: ReturnType<TradeNodesModel['getPlayerIntelligence']>;
            strategicValue: ReturnType<TradeNodesModel['assessStrategicValue']>;
            hubStatus: ReturnType<TradeNodesModel['isStrategicHub']>;
        };
        access: {
            permissionFlags: string[];
            allowsNeutral: boolean;
            allowsFaction: boolean;
            allowsAllies: boolean;
            allowsNPC: boolean;
            allowsEnemies: boolean;
        };
        recommendations: string[];
        overallRating: 'AVOID' | 'BASIC' | 'GOOD' | 'EXCELLENT' | 'PREMIUM';
    }
```

### TradeNodesModel.decodeItems

Decodes TRADE_NODES.ITEMS into a typed TradePricesObject.

The column stores zlib raw-DEFLATE compressed TradePrices data:
  int uncompressedSize + int deflatedSize + deflated payload
Each entry has: type (signed: negative=buy), blockType, amount, price, limit.

Returns null when the column is null, empty, or malformed.

[Source](../../src/tables/trade-nodes/TradeNodesModel.ts#L1131)

```typescript
public decodeItems(): import('starmade-decoder').TradePricesObject | null
```

- **@example** const prices = node.decodeItems(); if (prices) {   console.log(prices.buyOrders.length, prices.sellOrders.length);   const entry = prices.getBuyOrder(259); // blockType 259 = Faction Module   // Immutable mutation + write-back:   const updated = prices.withBuyOrder(259, 100, 500);   node.setItems(updated.toBytes()); }

### TradeNodesModel.encodeItems

Encodes and stores TRADE_NODES.ITEMS from a TradePricesObject or raw
TradePrices structure.

[Source](../../src/tables/trade-nodes/TradeNodesModel.ts#L1146)

```typescript
public encodeItems(
        prices: import('starmade-decoder').TradePricesObject | import('starmade-decoder').TradePrices
    ): this
```

- **@example** const prices = node.decodeItems(); if (prices) node.encodeItems(prices.withBuyOrder(259, 100, 500));

### VisibilityModel

Model for the VISIBILITY table

Strategic intelligence table implementing the "fog of war" system.
Tracks which sectors have been explored by specific players or factions,
creating a persistent record of spatial knowledge and reconnaissance activities.

```typescript
public generateIntelligenceReport(): {
        basic: {
            coordinates: string;
            observerId: number;
            observerType: string;
            observerName: string;
            timestamp: number | undefined;
            relativeTime: string;
            isRecent: boolean;
        };
        sector: {
            intelligence: ReturnType<VisibilityModel['getSectorIntelligence']>;
            protection: ReturnType<VisibilityModel['getSectorProtectionStatus']>;
        };
        assessment: {
            value: ReturnType<VisibilityModel['assessObservationValue']>;
            threat: ReturnType<VisibilityModel['isPotentialThreat']>;
        };
        recommendations: string[];
        overallRating: 'ROUTINE' | 'INTERESTING' | 'IMPORTANT' | 'CRITICAL';
    }
```

```typescript
        formattedTimestamp: string | null;
        isObserved: boolean;
        isInFogOfWar: boolean;

        isRecent: boolean;

        isNPCObservation: boolean;
        isSystemObservation: boolean;

        sectorIntelligence?: ReturnType<VisibilityModel['getSectorIntelligence']>;
        protectionStatus?: ReturnType<VisibilityModel['getSectorProtectionStatus']>;
        observationValue?: ReturnType<VisibilityModel['assessObservationValue']>;
        threatAssessment?: ReturnType<VisibilityModel['isPotentialThreat']>;
    }
```
